	tile_slot BC {
		bel_slot BCINRD: legacy;
		bel_slot BCLVDSO: legacy;
		bel_slot BCPG: legacy;
		bel_slot BCSLEWRATE: legacy;

		tile_class BC {
			cell CELL;

			bel BCINRD {
				input INRDENI = IMUX_C[5];
			}

			bel BCPG {
				input PGENI = IMUX_D[5];
